## 任务：在 cqa1-22115 cuda:0 上运行 exp-018 LLaMA-3.1-8B depth chain

### 目标
LLaMA-3.1-8B continuation mode depth chain generation + feature extraction + classification。首次跨模型家族 K* 验证。

### 服务器信息
- 服务器：cqa1-22115
- GPU：cuda:0（RTX 4090D 24GB）
- 环境：conda base（`source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base`）
- 项目路径：/root/autodl-tmp/gen-depth-contamination
- AutoDL：先 `source /etc/network_turbo`，设 HF_HOME=/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache, HF_HUB_DISABLE_XET=1, REQUESTS_CA_BUNDLE + SSL_CERT_FILE

### 模型信息
- LLaMA-3.1-8B 已下载（30GB）在 /root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache/
- 确认模型路径：`find /root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache -maxdepth 3 -name "config.json" | grep -i llama`
- 8B fp16 ~16GB，4090D 24GB 足够

### 关键：scorer 与 generator 不同
exp-018 的 scorer 应为 Qwen2.5-1.5B（base），不是 LLaMA 本身。检查 run_pipeline.py 是否支持 --scorer_path 参数。如果不支持，需要修改脚本或用两步流程：
1. 用 LLaMA-3.1-8B 生成 depth chain
2. 用 Qwen2.5-1.5B 做 feature extraction（surprisal 计算）

先检查 run_pipeline.py 的参数（ssh_read_only）：
```bash
python /root/autodl-tmp/gen-depth-contamination/scripts/run_pipeline.py --help 2>/dev/null || head -80 /root/autodl-tmp/gen-depth-contamination/scripts/run_pipeline.py
```

如果 pipeline 自动用 --model_path 作为 scorer，那需要修改——为 cross-family 实验，scorer 必须固定为 Qwen2.5-1.5B，否则不同模型间不可比。

**如果需要修改脚本**：添加 `--scorer_path` 参数，默认等于 `--model_path`（向后兼容），generation 用 model_path，feature extraction 的 surprisal 用 scorer_path。

### 提交
用 submit_training_job 提交，参数：
- exp_id: exp_018_llama8b_depth
- project: gen-depth-contamination
- server: cqa1-22115
- gpu_ids: ["cuda:0"]
- command: 确定后的完整命令

提交后用 update_registry 补充 spec（model_name, data_path, data_count, seed, scorer 等）。

### 验证
提交后确认：
- tmux session 创建成功
- 日志开始写入
- GPU 被占用（nvidia-smi 显示 cuda:0 有进程）

### 注意
- **8B 模型 fp16 约 16GB 显存**，确保不会 OOM
- batch_size 可能需要从 16 降低到 8（8B 模型更大）
- LLaMA tokenizer 可能需要设置 padding token（LLaMA 系列没有默认 pad_token）

{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"\\nMVP Pilot Saturation Study — Complete Pipeline (Steps 2-5)\\n\\nGenerates recursive depth chains from C4 seed data using Qwen2.5-1.5B,\\nextracts statistical features, and runs distinguishability analysis.\\n\\nInput:  C4 validation split (streamed from HuggingFace)\\nOutput: data/depth_{0..K}.jsonl, data/features.csv, results/*.json + summary.txt\\n\\nKey deps: torch, transformers, datasets, scipy, lightgbm, sklearn, tqdm\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nimport argparse\\nimport json\\nimport logging\\nimport math\\nimport os\\nimport sys\\nfrom collections import Counter\\nfrom pathlib import Path\\n\\nimport numpy as np\\nimport torch\\nfrom scipy import stats\\nfrom tqdm import tqdm\\n\\nlogging.basicConfig(\\n    level=logging.INFO,\\n    format=\\\"%(asctime)s [%(levelname)s] %(message)s\\\",\\n    datefmt=\\\"%H:%M:%S\\\",\\n)\\nlog = logging.getLogger(__name__)\\n\\n\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n# Utility: simple 4-gram BLEU (no nltk dependency)\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n\\ndef ngrams(tokens, n):\\n    return [tuple(tokens[i : i + n]) for i in range(len(tokens) - n + 1)]\\n\\n\\ndef simple_bleu(candidate_tokens, reference_tokens, max_n=4):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Compute sentence-level BLEU up to max_n grams with brevity penalty.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    if len(candidate_tokens) == 0:\\n        return 0.0\\n    precisions = []\\n    for n in range(1, max_n + 1):\\n        cand_ngrams = ngrams(candidate_tokens, n)\\n        ref_ngrams = ngrams(reference_tokens, n)\\n        if not cand_ngrams:\\n            precisions.append(0.0)\\n            continue\\n        ref_counts = Counter(ref_ngrams)\\n        clipped = 0\\n        cand_counts = Counter(cand_ngrams)\\n        for ng, cnt in cand_counts.items():\\n            clipped += min(cnt, ref_counts.get(ng, 0))\\n        precisions.append(clipped / len(cand_ngrams))\\n    if any(p == 0 for p in precisions):\\n        return 0.0\\n    log_avg = sum(math.log(p) for p in precisions) / max_n\\n    bp = min(1.0, math.exp(1 - len(reference_tokens) / max(len(candidate_tokens), 1)))\\n    return bp * math.exp(log_avg)\\n\\n\\n# ===================================================================\\n# Step 2: Seed data preparation\\n# ===================================================================\\n\\ndef prepare_seed_data(tokenizer, data_dir: Path, num_samples: int, max_tokens: int = 256):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Download C4 validation, truncate to max_tokens, save as depth_0.jsonl.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    out_path = data_dir / \\\"depth_0.jsonl\\\"\\n    if out_path.exists() and sum(1 for _ in open(out_path)) >= num_samples:\\n        log.info(\\\"depth_0.jsonl already exists with enough samples — skipping\\\")\\n        return\\n\\n    from datasets import load_dataset\\n\\n    # Use a single validation shard to avoid slow full-tree listing on proxied networks\\n    hf_endpoint = os.environ.get(\\\"HF_ENDPOINT\\\", \\\"https://huggingface.co\\\")\\n    C4_SHARD_URL = f\\\"{hf_endpoint}/datasets/allenai/c4/resolve/main/en/c4-validation.00000-of-00008.json.gz\\\"\\n    log.info(\\\"Loading C4 validation shard (streaming) ...\\\")\\n    try:\\n        ds = load_dataset(\\\"json\\\", data_files=C4_SHARD_URL, split=\\\"train\\\", streaming=True)\\n    except Exception as e:\\n        log.warning(f\\\"Direct shard download failed ({e}), falling back to full dataset listing\\\")\\n        ds = load_dataset(\\\"allenai/c4\\\", \\\"en\\\", split=\\\"validation\\\", streaming=True)\\n\\n    records = []\\n    for i, example in enumerate(tqdm(ds, total=num_samples, desc=\\\"Sampling C4\\\")):\\n        if i >= num_samples:\\n            break\\n        text = example[\\\"text\\\"].strip()\\n        if not text:\\n            continue\\n        ids = tokenizer.encode(text, add_special_tokens=False)[:max_tokens]\\n        truncated = tokenizer.decode(ids, skip_special_tokens=True)\\n        records.append({\\\"text\\\": truncated, \\\"doc_id\\\": len(records)})\\n        if len(records) >= num_samples:\\n            break\\n\\n    data_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\\n    with open(out_path, \\\"w\\\") as f:\\n        for r in records:\\n            f.write(json.dumps(r, ensure_ascii=False) + \\\"\\\\n\\\")\\n    log.info(f\\\"Saved {len(records)} seed documents to {out_path}\\\")\\n\\n\\n# ===================================================================\\n# Step 3: Recursive depth chain generation\\n# ===================================================================\\n\\nREWRITE_PROMPT = (\\n    \\\"Below is a passage. Write a new passage of similar length on the same \\\"\\n    \\\"topic and in the same style.\\\\n\\\\n\\\"\\n    \\\"Original passage:\\\\n{prev_text}\\\\n\\\\nNew passage:\\\\n\\\"\\n)\\n\\n\\ndef load_jsonl(path: Path):\\n    with open(path) as f:\\n        return [json.loads(line) for line in f]\\n\\n\\ndef generate_depth_chain(\\n    model,\\n    tokenizer,\\n    data_dir: Path,\\n    max_depth: int,\\n    batch_size: int,\\n    max_new_tokens: int = 256,\\n    top_p: float = 0.95,\\n    temperature: float = 1.0,\\n    do_sample: bool = True,\\n    generation_mode: str = \\\"rewrite\\\",\\n):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Generate depths 1..max_depth via prompt-chain rewriting.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    for depth in range(1, max_depth + 1):\\n        out_path = data_dir / f\\\"depth_{depth}.jsonl\\\"\\n        prev_path = data_dir / f\\\"depth_{depth - 1}.jsonl\\\"\\n\\n        if out_path.exists() and sum(1 for _ in open(out_path)) >= sum(1 for _ in open(prev_path)):\\n            log.info(f\\\"depth_{depth}.jsonl already complete — skipping\\\")\\n            continue\\n\\n        prev_records = load_jsonl(prev_path)\\n        log.info(f\\\"Generating depth {depth} ({len(prev_records)} docs, batch_size={batch_size}) ...\\\")\\n\\n        results = []\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.93}}"}

      "result": "{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"\\nMVP Pilot Saturation Study — Complete Pipeline (Steps 2-5)\\n\\nGenerates recursive depth chains from C4 seed data using Qwen2.5-1.5B,\\nextracts statistical features, and runs distinguishability analysis.\\n\\nInput:  C4 validation split (streamed from HuggingFace)\\nOutput: data/depth_{0..K}.jsonl, data/features.csv, results/*.json + summary.txt\\n\\nKey deps: torch, transformers, datasets, scipy, lightgbm, sklearn, tqdm\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nimport argparse\\nimport json\\nimport logging\\nimport math\\nimport os\\nimport sys\\nfrom collections import Counter\\nfrom pathlib import Path\\n\\nimport numpy as np\\nimport torch\\nfrom scipy import stats\\nfrom tqdm import tqdm\\n\\nlogging.basicConfig(\\n    level=logging.INFO,\\n    format=\\\"%(asctime)s [%(levelname)s] %(message)s\\\",\\n    datefmt=\\\"%H:%M:%S\\\",\\n)\\nlog = logging.getLogger(__name__)\\n\\n\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n# Utility: simple 4-gram BLEU (no nltk dependency)\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n\\ndef ngrams(tokens, n):\\n    return [tuple(tokens[i : i + n]) for i in range(len(tokens) - n + 1)]\\n\\n\\ndef simple_bleu(candidate_tokens, reference_tokens, max_n=4):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Compute sentence-level BLEU up to max_n grams with brevity penalty.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    if len(candidate_tokens) == 0:\\n        return 0.0\\n    precisions = []\\n    for n in range(1, max_n + 1):\\n        cand_ngrams = ngrams(candidate_tokens, n)\\n        ref_ngrams = ngrams(reference_tokens, n)\\n        if not cand_ngrams:\\n            precisions.append(0.0)\\n            continue\\n        ref_counts = Counter(ref_ngrams)\\n        clipped = 0\\n        cand_counts = Counter(cand_ngrams)\\n        for ng, cnt in cand_counts.items():\\n            clipped += min(cnt, ref_counts.get(ng, 0))\\n        precisions.append(clipped / len(cand_ngrams))\\n    if any(p == 0 for p in precisions):\\n        return 0.0\\n    log_avg = sum(math.log(p) for p in precisions) / max_n\\n    bp = min(1.0, math.exp(1 - len(reference_tokens) / max(len(candidate_tokens), 1)))\\n    return bp * math.exp(log_avg)\\n\\n\\n# ===================================================================\\n# Step 2: Seed data preparation\\n# ===================================================================\\n\\ndef prepare_seed_data(tokenizer, data_dir: Path, num_samples: int, max_tokens: int = 256):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Download C4 validation, truncate to max_tokens, save as depth_0.jsonl.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    out_path = data_dir / \\\"depth_0.jsonl\\\"\\n    if out_path.exists() and sum(1 for _ in open(out_path)) >= num_samples:\\n        log.info(\\\"depth_0.jsonl already exists with enough samples — skipping\\\")\\n        return\\n\\n    from datasets import load_dataset\\n\\n    # Use a single validation shard to avoid slow full-tree listing on proxied networks\\n    hf_endpoint = os.environ.get(\\\"HF_ENDPOINT\\\", \\\"https://huggingface.co\\\")\\n    C4_SHARD_URL = f\\\"{hf_endpoint}/datasets/allenai/c4/resolve/main/en/c4-validation.00000-of-00008.json.gz\\\"\\n    log.info(\\\"Loading C4 validation shard (streaming) ...\\\")\\n    try:\\n        ds = load_dataset(\\\"json\\\", data_files=C4_SHARD_URL, split=\\\"train\\\", streaming=True)\\n    except Exception as e:\\n        log.warning(f\\\"Direct shard download failed ({e}), falling back to full dataset listing\\\")\\n        ds = load_dataset(\\\"allenai/c4\\\", \\\"en\\\", split=\\\"validation\\\", streaming=True)\\n\\n    records = []\\n    for i, example in enumerate(tqdm(ds, total=num_samples, desc=\\\"Sampling C4\\\")):\\n        if i >= num_samples:\\n            break\\n        text = example[\\\"text\\\"].strip()\\n        if not text:\\n            continue\\n        ids = tokenizer.encode(text, add_special_tokens=False)[:max_tokens]\\n        truncated = tokenizer.decode(ids, skip_special_tokens=True)\\n        records.append({\\\"text\\\": truncated, \\\"doc_id\\\": len(records)})\\n        if len(records) >= num_samples:\\n            break\\n\\n    data_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\\n    with open(out_path, \\\"w\\\") as f:\\n        for r in records:\\n            f.write(json.dumps(r, ensure_ascii=False) + \\\"\\\\n\\\")\\n    log.info(f\\\"Saved {len(records)} seed documents to {out_path}\\\")\\n\\n\\n# ===================================================================\\n# Step 3: Recursive depth chain generation\\n# {"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"\\nMVP Pilot Saturation Study — Complete Pipeline (Steps 2-5)\\n\\nGenerates recursive depth chains from C4 seed data using Qwen2.5-1.5B,\\nextracts statistical features, and runs distinguishability analysis.\\n\\nInput:  C4 validation split (streamed from HuggingFace)\\nOutput: data/depth_{0..K}.jsonl, data/features.csv, results/*.json + summary.txt\\n\\nKey deps: torch, transformers, datasets, scipy, lightgbm, sklearn, tqdm\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nimport argparse\\nimport json\\nimport logging\\nimport math\\nimport os\\nimport sys\\nfrom collections import Counter\\nfrom pathlib import Path\\n\\nimport numpy as np\\nimport torch\\nfrom scipy import stats\\nfrom tqdm import tqdm\\n\\nlogging.basicConfig(\\n    level=logging.INFO,\\n    format=\\\"%(asctime)s [%(levelname)s] %(message)s\\\",\\n    datefmt=\\\"%H:%M:%S\\\",\\n)\\nlog = logging.getLogger(__name__)\\n\\n\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n# Utility: simple 4-gram BLEU (no nltk dependency)\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n\\ndef ngrams(tokens, n):\\n    return [tuple(tokens[i : i + n]) for i in range(len(tokens) - n + 1)]\\n\\n\\ndef simple_bleu(candidate_tokens, reference_tokens, max_n=4):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Compute sentence-level BLEU up to max_n grams with brevity penalty.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    if len(candidate_tokens) == 0:\\n        return 0.0\\n    precisions = []\\n    for n in range(1, max_n + 1):\\n        cand_ngrams = ngrams(candidate_tokens, n)\\n        ref_ngrams = ngrams(reference_tokens, n)\\n        if not cand_ngrams:\\n            precisions.append(0.0)\\n            continue\\n        ref_counts = Counter(ref_ngrams)\\n        clipped = 0\\n        cand_counts = Counter(cand_ngrams)\\n        for ng, cnt in cand_counts.items():\\n            clipped += min(cnt, ref_counts.get(ng, 0))\\n        precisions.append(clipped / len(cand_ngrams))\\n    if any(p == 0 for p in precisions):\\n        return 0.0\\n    log_avg = sum(math.log(p) for p in precisions) / max_n\\n    bp = min(1.0, math.exp(1 - len(reference_tokens) / max(len(candidate_tokens), 1)))\\n    return bp * math.exp(log_avg)\\n\\n\\n# level=logging.INFO,\\n    format=\\\"%(asctime)s [%(levelname)s] %(message)s\\\",\\n    datefmt=\\\"%H:%M:%S\\\",\\n)\\nlog = logging.getLogger(__name__)\\n\\n\\n# simple_bleu(candidate_tokens, reference_tokens, max_n=4):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Compute sentence-level BLEU up to max_n grams with brevity penalty.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    if len(candidate_tokens) == 0:\\n        return 0.0\\n    precisions = []\\n    for n in range(1, max_n + 1):\\n        cand_ngrams = ngrams(candidate_tokens, n)\\n        ref_ngrams = ngrams(reference_tokens, n)\\n        if not cand_ngrams:\\n            precisions.append(0.0)\\n            continue\\n        ref_counts = Counter(ref_ngrams)\\n        clipped = 0\\n        cand_counts = Counter(cand_ngrams)\\n        for ng, cnt in cand_counts.items():\\n            clipped += min(cnt, ref_counts.get(ng, 0))\\n        precisions.append(clipped / len(cand_ngrams))\\n    if any(p == 0 for p in precisions):\\n        return 0.0\\n    log_avg = sum(math.log(p) for p in precisions) / max_n\\n    bp = min(1.0, math.exp(1 - len(reference_tokens) / max(len(candidate_tokens), 1)))\\n    return bp * math.exp(log_avg)\\n\\n\\n# <details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

````text
The log is still growing (4472 lines now, up from earlier). All requests have the same cursor, which is concerning. This looks like it might be stuck in an infinite loop fetching the same page. The C4 dataset tree listing is the fallback because the direct shard download failed.

Actually wait - looking at the code, it tried the direct shard URL first:
```python
C4_SHARD_URL = f"{hf_endpoint}/datasets/allenai/c4/resolve/main/en/c4-validation.00000-of-00008.json.gz"
ds = load_dataset("json", data_files=C4_SHARD_URL, split="train", streaming=True)


```

If that fails, it falls back to loading the full C4 dataset with the "en" split, which triggers a tree listing that paginates through thousands of files. The repeated cursor is suspicious - it's the same base64 value every time, suggesting the API might be stuck returning the same pagination token or hitting some kind of rate limit or bug.

C4's "en" split has over 1000 shards, so pagination should work in just a couple of pages, but the logs show thousands of identical requests. This looks like a pagination loop, probably triggered by the proxy setup. The process will either eventually complete or hang indefinitely.

I need to kill this and try a different approach - using direct shard URLs should bypass the pagination issue entirely. Let me check if the proxy supports the direct shard URL format.

```

python
hf_endpoint = os.environ.get("HF_ENDPOINT", "https://huggingface.co")
C4_SHARD_URL = f"{hf_endpoint}/datasets/allenai/c4/resolve/main/en/c4-validation.00000-of-00008.json.gz"
```

The script already attempts direct shard URLs, so if that failed, the proxy might not handle resolve endpoints properly. Before trying another workaround, let me check if previous experiments have already cached the C4 data somewhere on the server that I could reuse.
````

Though ideally for a fair cross-family comparison, I'd want the seed data to be identical across experiments, which means the truncation points would differ between the Qwen and LLaMA tokenizers.

Actually, thinking about this more practically: the pipeline's `prepare_seed_data` function truncates to 256 tokens using whatever tokenizer I provide, and stores the decoded text in the jsonl file. If I reuse the existing data that was truncated with Qwen's tokenizer, it'll still work fine with LLaMA as the generator - text is just text. The real benefit is that using consistent seed data across experiments actually improves comparability.

Let me verify the sample count first, then I'll kill the current process, copy over the existing depth_0.jsonl file, and restart the pipeline since it already has logic to skip that step if enough samples are present.
